Specifically, normalized a (sum1). For small δ with sum0, c(a+δ)≈c(a)+2(a*δ) (since δ*δ negligible). Let r_k = (a*δ)_k. We want reduce current peak levels c_k close to M. Solve:

||δ||_1 ≤ 1 (or L2)
sum δ=0
a+δ ≥0

We can make a more systematic local search:
- Identify set S of lags with c_k > M - tolerance.
- Compute for each index i a "cost" vector s_i(k)=a_{k-i} (with boundaries). Moving from i to j changes active lags by s_j - s_i.
- We want find pair (i,j) and ε that reduces max c. This is like choose i with high projection on active weighted peaks and j with low. We can compute scalar score_i = ∑_{k∈active} w_k a_{k-i}; choose high i as source, low i as destination. This is exactly gradient of linearized objective for weighted active set. Use weights λ_k from KKT (e.g. normalized c_k^p). Then gradient g_i = (a*λ)_i (twice). Moving mass down gradient (to low g) reduces weighted peaks. This is like mirror descent with gradient λ convolved with a. Previous SSD used top peaks soft weights. We can do better: Use weights solving dual KKT: Lagrange multipliers λ_k≥0 such that gradient λ*a is constant on support. For local optimization, update a_i ∝ exp(-η (λ*a)_i). This is cross-entropy / multiplicative weight. If λ is chosen to make λ*a flat over indices, that's KKT. We can iteratively estimate λ: Given a, peaks c_k; set λ_k proportional to violation of c_k relative target or c_k^p. Then update a. This is similar to repeated: λ = softmax(c)/?; gradient = λ*a; a multiplicatively adjust inverse gradient. This might converge faster and allow larger steps.

Let's formulate: Minimize F(a)=max_k c_k. At optimum, subgradient λ*a normalized has constant on support. We can use projected stochastic: 
1. Given a, compute c.
2. Choose λ_k ≥0 with ∑λ=1, emphasizing peaks: λ_k = (c_k - τ)_+ / ∑ or softmax(β(c_k-M_avg)) with soft threshold. 
3. Compute h = λ * a (linear convolution, only need n length? For i, h_i=∑_{k} λ_k a_{k-i}).
4. Update a_i ← a_i / (1+η h_i) or exp(-η(h_i-const)), then project nonnegative/normalize.
This is similar to intuiting to reduce positions that contribute to active lags.

But previous SSD used weights on peaks and gradient = 2∑w a_{k-i}; mirror descent. Maybe need better λ and adaptive step. We can implement a "dual ascent" for max: maximize over λ? Let's detail gradient optimizer.

Function lin_conv(a): returns c length 2n-1 using FFT.
- n = len(a)
- size=2n-1, fft_size=1<< (size-1).bit_length()
- A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
- return np.fft.irfft(A*A, fft_size)[:size]
(real, small negatives set 0)

Function weighted_gradient(a, w): h_i = ∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. Use N = 1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()? Need length N >= n + (2n-1) -1? For product W(z) A(z^{-1}), degree positive 2n-2, negative -(n-1). To get coefficients 0..n-1 no alias, N > (n-1)+(2n-2)+1? If using standard IFFT of product with sequences W positive indices 0..2n-2 and A_conj? Let's do empirically by constructing A_pad with length N, A_conj? We'll use DFT formula: A_fft = np.fft.fft(a, N) but a pad; W_fft = np.fft.fft(w, N). H = np.fft.ifft(np.conj(A_fft) * W_fft).real. Need N at least (n-1)+(2n-2)+1? The product in time domain (circular) of sequences a (indices 0..n-1) and flipped w? Wait FFT multiplication corresponds circular convolution: ifft(fft(x)*fft(y)) = x * y circular (with y positive index). We used conj(A_fft) because A_conj in frequency corresponds to time-domain a reversed? For real a, conj(FFT(a)) = FFT(a_rev?) Specifically if A_k=∑ a_j e^{-2πikj/N}, conj(A_k)=∑ a_j e^{+2πikj/N}=∑ a_j e^{-2πik(-j)/N}, which is DFT of sequence a_neg with nonzero at indices -j mod N. So product conj(A)W corresponds circular convolution of a_neg (indices N-j) and W (indices k). Coefficient at m =∑_j a_j W_{m+j mod N}? Because a_neg at N-j, convolution: ∑_{r} a_neg[r] W_{m-r}; r=N-j -> W_{m-N+j}=W_{m+j mod N}. If linear no wrap for m=0..n-1 and i+j within 0..2n-2, need N such that m+j not wrap and m+j max (n-1)+(2n-2)=3n-3 < N. Also negative indices wrap: r=N-j for j>0 near N, convolution with W index m+j positive; no issue if N >3n. So N >=3n-2 sufficient. Use next power of two >=3*n.
Then H[0:n] = h. Test small: a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2], N=8. a_neg indices 0:N? j=1 at N-1. Convolution coefficient m=0: a0w0 + a1w1 (from r=N-1, m-r=1 modN) good. m=1: a0w1 + a1w2. Good.

Softmax update: We maintain loga. Initially loga = log(a + eps). At each step:
- h = weighted_gradient(a, w)
- Scale h: To avoid huge steps, normalize h by its norm or variance. Let h_centered = h - h.mean(). h has units c ~1/n, so h ~1/n. In log space, step η may need ~ n. If loga update -η h, with h~1e-4, to get O(1) log change need η~10000. But we can scale h to have unit std: h_norm = h / (np.std(h)+eps). Then step η in O(1) maybe. However subtracting mean irrelevant. Use g = h - mean(h), scale = sqrt(mean(g^2)), g = g/scale. Then loga -= step * g. step by line search maybe 0.5-5.
- But if h constant, no change.
- Candidate a_new = exp(loga - maxlog)/sum.
- Evaluate M_new. We can do backtracking: try steps [base, base*0.5, base*0.25, ...] until M_new < M_best? But if using smoothed F (softmax), objective may increase transient. We can accept if M_new < current M (or <best). If none, try smaller or skip.
- We want fast convergence to max, so use current M not best? Using best may get stuck. Use current M for line search but keep global best.
- Update loga,a if accepted. If no improvement over several iterations, perturb: add random noise to loga or β change.

Implementation of LP shift:
```
def shifted_lp_direction(y, active, solver):
    n=len(y); c=lin_conv(y); c_act = c[active]
    K=len(active)
    A = np.zeros((K,n))
    for jj,k in enumerate(active): build row = 2*y[k - idx]
    g=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
    t=cp.Variable()
    constraints=[cp.sum(g)==1, A@g - t <= c_act/2, t >= -10*c.max()]
    prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), constraints)
    prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
    if infeasible: fallback
    gval=max(g.value,0); g/=sum
    return g,t
```
But A@g can be large; c_act/2 scale ~1/(2n). t predicted max ~1/n. Bounds okay.
Could omit t lower bound to avoid infeasible; but lower bound -M safe? Since optimum t≥? For feasible g, 2yg - c/2. Since 2yg≥0, t≥ -max(c/2) = -M/2? Actually constraint t ≥ 2yg - c/2 for all k; right side can be negative as low as -c/2 ≥ -M/2. So t ≥ -M/2. Add t >= -M maybe safe.

Line search:
```
def lp_step(y,g,t_pred):
    alphas = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]
    best = y, M
    for alpha in alphas:
       trial = (1-alpha)*y + alpha*g; normalize; M=lin_conv(trial).max(); keep min
    return trial
```

Now, could there be an even better approach using continuous optimal and concave transform? Let's further investigate mathematical lower bound and possible exact construction to guide. Maybe use sequences from error-correcting codes: Let a_i = 1/N on a set S of size N? If support full n, a uniform M=1 (J=2). To get M=0.75, need nonuniform. What if a_i = 1/n on all i except some zeros/weights according to a difference set? In cyclic group, if a is uniform on a subset S of size s in group size L, convolution max = |S| (or |S|-1) for lags in S-S? For linear? Suppose choose a as 0/1 on n positions (sum=s) scaled 1/s. Objective =2n * max pairs count / s^2. We want max pairs count /s^2 minimized. For random 0/1 with density p=s/n, expected pairs ~ p^2 n for each lag, max ~ p^2 n log n. Objective ~2n p^2 n log n / (p^2 n^2)=2 log n. Bad. But structured difference set with max pairs ~s/L? If group size L~n, for a subset with difference set balanced, max pairs ~s^2/L. Objective ~2n * s^2/L / s^2 =2n/L ~2 if L=n. Not 1.5. Need weights not binary maybe.

Maybe use a_i proportional to sqrt of a flat convolution root. If target c flat, polynomial root has coefficients maybe nonnegative if target c is square of Fejér? Let's use finite Fourier to construct a by iterating: choose target c_k = 1/(2n-1) for k=1..2n-3, endpoints maybe smaller. Compute its DFT C. Need A such that A^2=C. Choose A_k = sqrt(|C_k|/|C_0|) * e^{i arg C_k /2}. Then a=IDFT(A). Project positive? This is like spectral square root. Could produce a with flat c if no projection. Let's test conceptually. C for nearly rectangular sequence (sum1) has C_0=1, other C_k = -? For rectangle length L=2n-1 with height 1/L, C_k = (1/L) e^{-ik(L-1)/2} 2 sin(kL/2)/sin(k/2)? L odd -> sin(kL/2)=sin(k n - k/2)? Not zero. Square root phases random; inverse may be nonnegative? Maybe after many iterations of projecting c to uniform and taking square root, we can get a good a. This is reminiscent of finding nonnegative sequence with prescribed power spectrum (phase retrieval). Could be faster and different.

Algorithm spectral_target:
- Start a positive (e.g. parent).
- Iterate:
  1. c=a*a (linear, zero-padded to L=2n-1, maybe embed in circular N≥L).
  2. Modify c to desired target: c_new = (1-damp)*target + damp*c? Ensure sum=1, nonnegative.
  3. Compute A = sqrt Fourier square root of c_new under linear convolution. For circular N≥2n? If use zero-padded length L=2n-1, polynomial P^2 = target polynomial truncated. def alt_projection(a, m, iters, damp):
  for it:
    c=lin_conv(a)
    # project c to max≤m and sum1
    c_new = np.minimum(c, m)
    s=c_new.sum()
    if s<=0: c_new = c.copy(); s=1
    c_new /= s
    # maybe smooth: c_new = c_new + alpha*(target_uniform?)
    a_new = fft_square_root(c_new, a)
    a_new = norm_sum1(max(a_new,0))
    # optionally keep a = blend
  return a
```

```
def project_c_max(c, m):
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    c_new = np.minimum(c, m)
    # iterative water fill
    for _ in range(100):
      s = c_new.sum()
      deficit = 1 - s
      if abs(deficit) <= 1e-15: break
      if deficit > 0:
         free = c_new < m - 1e-15
         cnt = free.sum()
         if cnt==0: break
         # We need distribute deficit increasing; to maintain close to c, maybe add proportionally to (m - c_new) for free? Simple uniform may change shape. Use add = min(deficit/cnt, min_margin)
         margin = m - c_new[free]
         max_uniform = deficit / cnt
         if max_uniform <= margin.min():
            c_new[free] += max_uniform
         else:
            # add to those with smallest margin up to min margin
            order = np.argsort(margin)
            # fill sequentially maybe O(K) but okay 13k*? We can vectorized accumulate.
            remaining=deficit
            for? Could do while but expensive.
      else:
         # if sum>1 (shouldn't), reduce equally from non-zero
    return c_new
```

def clip_sum_vector(c, m):
    c = np.maximum(c, 0).astype(float)
    c = np.minimum(c, m)
    # repeated: add to those below m uniformly until sum1
    # Because m > average, feasible.

        s = c.sum()
        deficit = 1 - s
        if deficit <= 1e-14: break
        if deficit > 0:
            free = c < m - 1e-15
            cnt = free.sum()
            if cnt == 0:

            # sum >1, reduce non-zero proportionally? Shouldn't happen if all ≤m and average<m.

This is a Newton step for the convolution equation, enforcing length n by taking real part. This may be more appropriate than full square root and uses current a. Let's derive in polynomial ring with zero padding N≥Nc. Let A=FFT(a padded N), C=FFT(c), Ctar=FFT(c_target). Newton for F(a)=a^2-Ctar=0: a_new = a - F / F'(a) = a - (a^2-C)/(2a) in ring. In frequency: A_new = A - (A^2 - Ctar)/(2A) = (A + Ctar/A)/2. This is the standard Newton square root in frequency! It uses current A as initial and produces A_new frequency. It preserves Hermitian if Ctar real and A real? A may be complex. A_new = 0.5*(A + Ctar/A). This converges to a root whose frequencies are chosen near current A (branch via current). Then b=ifft(A_new).real. We can take first n. This is better than principal sqrt and aligns branch with current. It also naturally length? A_new is full length N; if exact root length n, high freq correspond to coefficients beyond n? Actually a length n padded, A is DFT of length n with zeros after n. Newton root may produce full N coefficients; we truncate to n. But if current a length n, the iteration in truncated ring? We can after ifft take first n and discard tail; this is like projecting to length n. This could work and avoids sign issues. We can do one or several Newton frequency steps per projection iteration.

Algorithm alternating Newton:
- a current normalized (length n), A=FFT(a,N).
- c=a*a, C=FFT(c,N), Ctar=FFT(projected_c,N).
- Newton step(s): for s in steps: A = 0.5*(A + Ctar/A) (where A zeros? avoid divide by zero: add small epsilon with phase? If |A|<eps, use sign? We can set A[|A|<eps] = eps + 0j).
- b=irfft? A may not be Hermitian exactly; use real ifft. Since Ctar Hermitian and initial A from real a, Newton preserves Hermitian approximately. We can enforce A = (A + conj(A[::-1]))/2.
- b_full=ifft(A).real. Take a_new = b_full[:n]. (Maybe if a0 should be sqrt(c0), but normalized sum later).
- Project a_new nonnegative: a_new = max(a_new,0); normalize. Blend with current if needed.

This is elegant! It is like alternating projections: project c to feasible box, then take Newton square root in frequency aligned with current a. At a fixed point with a length n and c≤m, c_target=c, A root of C with A=FFT(a padded); Newton A=(A+C/A)/2=A, b_full=a (zeros tail), fixed. Great.

This could be very powerful and different from previous. Need implement carefully.

1. Utility:
```
def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
def norm_sum1(a): ...
def lin_conv(a): ...
def lin_conv_max(a): return lin_conv(a).max()
def local_score(a): return 2.0*len(a)*lin_conv_max(a)
def interp(a,new_n): ...
```
`lin_conv` use rfft: `size = len(a)*2-1; N=1<<(size-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N); return c[:size]`. Clip negatives: `c[c<0 & c>-1e-15]=0`; maybe `return np.maximum(c,0)`.

2. Project c to box sum:
```
def project_box_sum(c, m):
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c,dtype=float),0.0)
    # if sum zero return uniform
    s=c.sum(); if s<1e-15: return np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    c = c/s
    if m <= 0: return c
    # if c.max() <= m*(1+1e-12) and abs(s-1)<... return c
    if c.max() <= m*(1+1e-12): return c
    # Bisection for lambda: p=clip(c - lam, 0, m)
    lo = c.min() - m - max(1.0, c.max())
    hi = c.max() + max(1.0, c.max())
    # We need sum p =1. sum decreases with lam.
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       p=np.clip(c-mid,0.0,m)
       s=p.sum()
       if s > 1.0:
          hi = mid
       else:
          lo = mid
    p=np.clip(c-lo,0.0,m)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-15: p=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    else: p /= s
    # Due numerical, max may exceed m slightly; min
    p = np.minimum(p, m)
    p /= p.sum()
    # If max still > m due infeasible? But m > avg so should not.
    return p
```
Need verify monotonic direction: c=[1.2,0.2,0.2,0.4] sum2? normalized? Let's use sum1 with peak>m. Suppose c=[0.6,0.4], m=0.5. Feasible p? Box [0,0.5], sum1 => only [0.5,0.5]. p(λ)=clip(c-λ,0,0.5). If λ=-0.1: c-lam=[0.7,0.5] clip [0.5,0.5] sum1. Good. hi? c.max=0.6. Need λ=-0.1 <0. lo negative. If s>1, λ too low? At λ=-1, p=[0.5,0.5] sum1? At λ=-2, c+2=[2.6,2.4] clip sum1? Actually cap 0.5 both =>1, always for λ very negative if number caps enough. If number of caps *m may be >1, for λ super negative sum = #closed? Could be >1 if fewer caps? Example L=10 m=0.2 sum1, need 5 caps at 0.2. For λ very negative, all 10 caps sum2>1. As λ increases, sum decreases. So if s>1, need increase λ (hi=mid if mid maybe low? Let's set lo very low => s large; hi very high => s small. If s>1, move lo=mid? Wait if s>1 at mid, need larger λ, so set lo=mid (raise lower bound). If s<1, need smaller λ, set hi=mid. In code above I had if s>1 hi=mid incorrectly. Let's fix:
- range lo=min_c - m -1 (very negative), hi=max_c +1 (very positive).
- f(λ)=sum clip(c-λ,0,m), decreasing in λ.
- Want f=1. If f(mid) >1, need λ larger => lo=mid. If f(mid) <1, need λ smaller => hi=mid.
At end use lo or midpoint. Good.
But for c=[0.6,0.4], m=0.5: lo negative f=1? If lo=-1? f=1; hi=1.6 f=0. mid maybe0.3 f=0.2? then hi=0.3; eventually λ around -0.1. Need lo below -0.1. Good.

def newton_clip_root(a, m_target, time_limit, newton_iters=2, verbose=False):

4. Mirror gradient optimizer:
Use weights and FFT gradient. Implement efficiently.
```
def weighted_gradient(a, w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w) # 2n-1
    N=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()? Need safe: N=1<<(3*n - 2 -1).bit_length() maybe if 3n-2 =1? Use size=3*n-1, N=next_pow2(size)
    A=np.fft.fft(a,N)
    W=np.fft.fft(w,N)
    h=np.fft.ifft(np.conj(A)*W).real[:n]
    return h
```
Check N requirement: max i+j for h_i with i=n-1, j? w index k=i+j up to 3n-3. Need N >3n-3. Also negative wrap for r? N>=3n-2. Use `size = 3*n` safe. N=next_pow2(size).

Mirror function:
```
def mirror_max_descent(a0, time_limit, beta_schedule, step0, restarts):
    n; loga = log(norm_sum1(a0)+1e-300)
    best...
    eta=step0
    no_improve=0
    for it:
       a=softmax(loga)
       c=lin_conv(a); M=c.max()
       update best
       # weights
       # q maybe from schedule; threshold margin
       w = make_weights(c, q, margin=...)
       h = weighted_gradient(a,w)
       g = h - h.mean()
       scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))
       if scale <1e-15: break
       g/=scale
       # line search
       for step in [eta, eta*0.7, eta*0.5, eta*0.3, eta*0.15]:
          new_loga = loga - step*g
          new_loga = clip(new_loga, clip_min, clip_max)
          new_a=softmax(new_loga)
          M_new=lin_conv_max(new_a)
          if M_new < M - 1e-15: accept; break
       else:
          # reduce eta, maybe perturb
          eta *=0.8; no_improve++
       if accepted:
          loga=new_loga; if improvement small eta*=1.05 else? no_improve=0
       if no_improve > restart_every:
          loga = log(best) + noise*0.02; q adjust; eta=step0
```
Need `make_weights(c,q,margin)`:
Option 1 threshold: floor = M - delta*M, delta=0.01? w = zeros; active=c>floor; if none active top1; w[active]=(c[active]-floor)**q (or c**q). Normalize.
For q, start 1, increase to 50. If q large, top. Use `if q > 20`: hard top K = max(10, int(n*0.001)?).
Maybe use softmax: `w = np.exp((c - M) * beta)`; beta from 10/n to 200/n? Since c-M scale 1/n, beta = λ/M? Use q formulation easier: `w = (c/M)^q`; with c/M ∈[0,1], q large. This weights by relative magnitude; low lags can contribute if q small. Normalize. If q=2, many; q=100 top.
We can select q adaptively: if no improve, increase q (focus peaks); if many decreases, lower q (broader).
```
def make_weights(c,q):
    M=c.max()
    if M<=0: return ones/len
    w=(c/max(M,1e-30))**q
    w += 1e-30
    return w/w.sum()
```
For q=50, values below0.9 underflow; okay.

This mirror update may be too slow if each iteration 2 FFTs (c and maybe gradient) plus line search multiple. But N for gradient 32768, c 16384. Could do 1000 iterations in tens seconds.

Potential performance concerns:
- Newton project_box_sum with bisection 80*len(c) each iter may be heavy if many iterations. We can optimize: Since c sum1 and m fixed, if current M close to m, many lags below m. Let's detail each function.

`next_pow2(x):`
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()
```

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a) / len(a)
    return a / s
```
If a has nan? We can replace nan: `a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)`. Use in initial.

`lin_conv`:
```
def lin_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(size)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:size]
    c = np.real_if_close(c, tol=1000).astype(np.float64)
    # fix small neg
    c[(c < 0.0) & (c > -1e-14)] = 0.0
    # if large negatives? max with0? The true c nonnegative; FFT errors can be small. Use np.maximum(c,0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    # normalize sum? Due errors sum maybe 0.999999; but for objective max only. Could normalize to sum1 to be consistent? If a sum1, c sum should1. FFT errors can make sum off 1e-15. We can leave.
    return c
```
Using `np.maximum` could hide significant negative if a negative? a nonnegative. Good.

`lin_conv_max`: returns float(c.max()).

`official_score`:
```
def official_score(a):
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
```
If evaluate_sequence not defined? It is imported. okay.

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
    f=np.asarray(f,float); old=f.size
    if old==new_n: return f.copy()
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    fn=f/s
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,fn)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    ss=out.sum()
    if ss<=1e-15: out=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    else: out/=ss
    return out
```
This preserves sum1. For large old,new, linspace memory okay (20k).

`project_box_sum`:
Need bisection carefully. If m <=0 return uniform? If m < 1/len(c) infeasible; but target m > avg. If c.max <= m*(1+1e-12) return c normalized.
```
def project_box_sum(c,m):
    c=np.maximum(np.asarray(c,float),0.0)
    s=c.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    c/=s
    if m <= 0: return c
    if c.max() <= m*(1+1e-12): return c
    # If m*len(c) <=1: infeasible, return uniform?
    if m*c.size <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        # use as flat as possible: uniform?
        return np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    lo = float(c.min()) - m - max(1.0, float(c.max()))
    hi = float(c.max()) + max(1.0, float(c.max()))
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       p=np.clip(c-mid,0.0,m)
       sp=p.sum()
       if sp > 1.0:
          lo = mid
       else:
          hi = mid
    p=np.clip(c-lo,0.0,m)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       p=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    else:
       p/=s
    # ensure max<=m: if slight over, clip and renorm
    p=np.minimum(p,m)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: p=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    else: p/=s
    return p
```
Check bisection direction with example: c=[0.6,0.4] m=0.5. lo negative, hi 1.6. mid ~0.3, p=[0.3,0.1] sum0.4<1 => hi=0.3. mid ~ -0.1, p sum1 -> if >1 lo=mid else hi. Converges. Good.

Newton improve:
```
def newton_improve(a0, time_limit, target_score, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=a.copy(); bestM=lin_conv_max(a)
    t0=time.monotonic(); best_official? not here
    frac=2e-4
    stall=0
    rho_list=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.07)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(101)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       c=lin_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
       if M < bestM - 1e-15:
          bestM=M; best=a.copy(); stall=0
       target_M = target_score/(2.0*n)
       if M <= target_M * (1.0 + 1e-12):
          # try to improve a bit below target? use dynamic target lower to squeeze
          target_M = max(0.0, M - 1e-6*M)
       m_target = min(M*(1.0 - frac), target_M)
       if m_target <= 0 or m_target >= M:
          # if target close, use small reduction
          m_target = M * max(1.0 - frac, 0.9999)
       c_target=project_box_sum(c, m_target)
       C=np.fft.fft(c_target, N)
       A=np.fft.fft(a, N)
       # Newton 2 steps
       for _ in range(2):
           absA=np.abs(A)
           small = absA < 1e-10
           A_safe = A.copy()
           if np.any(small):
               phase = np.ones(N, dtype=np.complex128)
               nz = absA > 0
               phase[nz] = A[nz] / absA[nz]
               A_safe = A.copy()
               A_safe[small] = (1e-10 + 0j) * phase[small]
           else:
               A_safe = A
           A = 0.5 * (A + C / A_safe)
           A[0] = 1.0 + 0j
       b=np.fft.ifft(A).real
       a_cand = b[:n]
       a_cand = np.maximum(a_cand, 0.0)
       ss=a_cand.sum()
       if ss <= 1e-12:
           a_cand = a.copy()
       else:
           a_cand = a_cand / ss
       accepted = False
       current_M = M
       for rho in rho_list:
           trial = (1.0-rho)*a + rho*a_cand
           trial = norm_sum1(trial)
           M2=lin_conv_max(trial)
           if M2 < current_M - 1e-16:
               a=trial; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           # if big improvement, keep frac; if small, tighten
           M_after = lin_conv_max(a)
           if M_after < bestM - 1e-15:
              bestM=M_after; best=a.copy()
               if M_after - target_M > 1e-8:
                   frac = max(frac*0.8, 1e-5)
               else:
                   frac = max(frac*0.6, 1e-6)
           else:
               frac = max(frac*0.8, 1e-6)
           stall=0
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall < 5:
               frac = min(frac*2.0, 1e-2)
           else:
               # perturb
               noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02, n) * a
               a = norm_sum1(a + noise)
               frac = min(frac*2.0, 1e-2)
               stall=0
       it += 1
       if verbose and (it % 50 == 0 or it == 1):
           print("    Newton iter=" + str(it) + " M=" + repr(M) + " frac=" + repr(frac))
    return best
```
Concern: If target_M is lower than M, m_target may be M*(1-frac) until close. Good. But `target_M = target_score/(2n)` if target_score is 1.5027 corresponds M_target ~0.000112. If current M=0.0001123, m_target=M*(1-2e-4)=0.00011217, above target. Good.

Line search tolerance `1e-16`: M ~1e-4, 1e-16 absolute maybe below numerical noise. Use `1e-14`? If too strict, may reject tiny improvements. Use `tol = 1e-15`. But FFT noise maybe 1e-19? okay. For target diff 1e-10, need accept improvements >1e-12. Use `tol = 1e-14` perhaps. Let's set `tol = 1e-14`. Actually M diff for J diff 0.001 is 7e-8? Wait recalc: n=6700, J diff 0.001 => M diff = 0.001/(13400)=7.5e-8. M~1.1e-4, fraction 6e-4. Sorry earlier I said 1e-10 erroneously using 0.001? Let's compute: 0.001/13400 = 7.46e-8. Yes. Previous diff to target 1.504047-1.5027=0.001347 => M diff 1.0e-7? 0.001347/13400=1.005e-7. Fraction 0.0009. So improvements of 1e-8 easy. Tolerance 1e-12 fine. Good.

Newton candidate M2 might be slightly higher due FFT. Use tol 1e-15.

`official_score` at phases may produce best. But newton returns local best.

Mirror improve: Need implement with target. Let's write robust.
```
def mirror_improve(a0, time_limit, target_score, q_start=2.0, q_end=80.0, step0=0.2, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a)
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
    best=a.copy(); bestM=lin_conv_max(a)
    t0; eta=step0; q=q_start; stall=0; it=0
    target_base=target_score/(2*n)
    rng=default_rng(202)
    clip_min=-50; clip_max=50
    steps=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.07)
    while time...:
       a=np.exp(loga-loga.max()); a/=a.sum(); a=np.maximum(a,0)
       c=lin_conv(a); M=c.max()
       if M<bestM-1e-14: best...
       if M <= target_base*(1+1e-12):
           target_M = max(0.0, M - 1e-5*M)
       else:
           target_M = target_base
       floor = min(target_M, M - 1e-8) if target_M < M else M - 0.005*M
       if floor < 0: floor = 0.0
       w = np.maximum(c - floor, 0.0)
       w = w ** q
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws < 1e-300:
          w = (c / max(M,1e-300)) ** q
          ws=w.sum()
       w = w / ws
       h=weighted_gradient(a,w)
       g=h - float(np.mean(h))
       scale=np.sqrt(float(np.mean(g*g)))
       if scale <1e-15: break
       g /= scale
       accepted=False
       for s in steps:
           new_loga = loga - eta*s*g
           new_loga = np.clip(new_loga, clip_min, clip_max)
           na=np.exp(new_loga-new_loga.max()); na/=na.sum()
           M2=lin_conv_max(na)
           if M2 < M - 1e-15:
               loga=new_loga; a=na; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if M2 < bestM - 1e-14: best...
           eta = min(eta*1.05, 3.0)
           q = max(q_start, q*0.95) # broaden a bit
           stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
           eta *= 0.8
           q = min(q*1.15, q_end)
           if stall > 100:
              # restart from best with noise
              base=best if bestM < M else a
              base=norm_sum1(base)
              loga=np.log(base + rng.normal(0,0.01, n)*base + 1e-300)
              loga=np.clip(loga, clip_min, clip_max)
              a=np.exp(loga-loga.max()); a/=a.sum()
              eta=step0; q=q_start; stall=0
       it+=1
       if verbose and it%100==0: print...
    return best
```
Potential issue: `target_M` dynamic below target_base when M <= target_base. If target score 1.5027 and current M lower, it will keep trying lower; good. But if true optimum near target, may chase noise. Use small `M - 1e-5M`. Good.

`weighted_gradient`: Use N=next_pow2(3*n). We can optimize by precomputing N each call? n constant per function; but helper generic. okay.
```
def weighted_gradient(a,w):
    n=a.size; N=next_pow2(3*n)
    A=np.fft.fft(a,N); W=np.fft.fft(w,N)
    h=np.fft.ifft(np.conj(A)*W).real[:n]
    return h
```
Check if N=3n is enough: max i+j = 3n-3? If n=1, N=3. okay. If N=3n, >3n-3. Negative wrap? r=-j min -(n-1), k max 2n-2, coefficient index m =? Our derivation with convolution a_neg (indices N-j) and W(k). For m=0..n-1, m+j (mod N) max n-1+2n-2=3n-3<N. Good. low? r=N-j large; m-r mod N = m+j. Need m+j <N. good.

`cross_conv`:
```
def cross_conv(a,b):
    n=a.size; size=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(size); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:size]; return np.maximum(c,0)
```

`get_solver`:

def get_solver():
    global _module_solver
    if _module_solver is not None: return _module_solver
    if cp is None: return None
    for s in [cp.PDLP, cp.CLARABEL, cp.ECOS, cp.SCS]:
       try:
          x=cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
          prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x)==1, x<=0.5])
          prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
          if prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'):
             _module_solver=s; print; return s
       except: continue
    try: 
       x=cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True); prob=...
       prob.solve(); if status...
    except: pass
    return None

`shifted_lp_fw`:
```
def shifted_lp_fw(a0, time_limit, solver, K=300, maxK=800, verbose=True):
    if solver is None: return a0
    y=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(y)
    best=y.copy(); bestM=lin_conv_max(y)
    t0; active_set=None; score=None
    K_cur=K if K<n//2 else max(50,n//10)
    no_improve=0; it=0
    while time...:
       c=lin_conv(y); M=c.max()
       if M<bestM-1e-14: best...
       if active_set is None:
          idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K_cur]
          active_set=set(int(k) for k in idx)
          score=c.copy()
       else:
          # update score decay and active
          # compute pred? We need previous g? Could just update with c.
          score = 0.9*score + 0.1*c
          # select candidates: top score plus top c
          # To ensure K_cur constraints
          idx=np.argsort(score)[::-1][:K_cur*2]
          # add top c
          topc=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K_cur]
          cand=set(int(k) for k in idx) | set(int(k) for k in topc)
          # score candidates, keep top K_cur
          if len(cand)>K_cur:
             arr=np.array(sorted(cand))
             vals=score[arr]
             order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:K_cur]
             cand=set(int(arr[i]) for i in order)
          active_set=cand
       active_list=sorted(active_set)
       if not active_list: break
       # maybe cap K_cur growth
       A=np.zeros((len(active_list), n), dtype=np.float64)
       c_act=np.array([c[k] for k in active_list], dtype=np.float64)
       for jj,k in enumerate(active_list):
           i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n,k+1)
           if i0 < i1:
              start=k - i0
              stop=k - i1
              A[jj,i0:i1] = 2.0 * y[start:stop:-1]
       g=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
       t=cp.Variable()
       constraints=[cp.sum(g)==1, A@g - t <= c_act/2.0, t >= -M]
       prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), constraints)
       try: prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
       except Exception: prob.status='error'
       if prob.status not in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'): 
           # reduce K
           K_cur=max(100, K_cur//2)
           continue
       gval=np.maximum(np.asarray(g.value, dtype=np.float64),0.0)
       s= gval.sum()
       if s <=1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): gval=y.copy(); s=1.0
       else: gval /= s
       # predicted
       conv_yg = cross_conv(y,gval)
       pred = 2.0*conv_yg - c
       # line search
       alphas = (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.125, max(0.01, 2.0/(it+2.0)), 0.05)
       best_trial=y; best_trial_M=M
       for alpha in alphas:
          trial=(1-alpha)*y + alpha*gval
          trial=norm_sum1(trial)
          M2=lin_conv_max(trial)
          if M2 < best_trial_M - 1e-15:
             best_trial=trial; best_trial_M=M2
       if best_trial_M < M - 1e-15:
          y=best_trial
          if y? update best
          no_improve=0
          # update score with pred
          score = np.maximum(0.8*score + 0.2*c, pred)
          # grow K slowly
          K_cur = min(maxK, K_cur + 10)
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          # update score with pred maybe
          score = np.maximum(0.8*score + 0.2*c, pred)
          if no_improve > 30:
              # reduce K and reselect
              K_cur = max(100, K_cur//2)
              active_set=None
          if no_improve > 300: break
       it+=1
       if verbose and it%20==0: print...
    return best
```
Need be careful: `score` can become large from pred negative? pred can be negative (predicted decrease). We want high constraints that are tight; use `score = np.maximum(0.8*score + 0.2*c, np.maximum(pred, 0.0))` maybe. If pred negative, don't boost. Use `pred_pos = np.maximum(pred, 0)`. Since pred can be below c. Good.
`active_set` from top score: if pred for some constraints negative but c high, c keeps. If new violations high pred positive, included.
Could K_cur exceed n? cap maxK. For n small, K_cur=min(maxK,n//2). Use if n<200 maybe skip LP.

LP constraint RHS: `A@g - t <= c_act/2`. If t is negative, okay. Lower bound -M. But if c_act/2 small, A@g nonnegative, t must be at least A@g - small. Feasible t maybe positive. Good.

Does minimizing t with lower bound -M allow t less than true predicted max? Constraints enforce t ≥ A@g - c/2 for active. For inactive not enforced. So t is max active linearized. Good.

Line search full step might still increase due second order; handle.

Potential performance: In each LP, building A loops over K. For K=500,n=6700, okay. But `A@g - t <= c_act/2` with dense A will create problem matrix maybe. cvxpy may take seconds. We can reduce K to 300 for final. Start K=200, max 600. Maybe enough. Previous used 300-2000 but achieved 1.504. Our shifted LP may need fewer.

`coordinate_polish`:
Implement a simple gradient pair move.
```
def coordinate_polish(a0,time_limit,verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); best=a; bestM=M; t0; rng; stall=0; it=0
    while time...:
       c=lin_conv(a); M=c.max(); update best
       if M <= target? no target; continue until stall.
       top = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:20]
       # weights softmax of top by c relative
       w_top = (c[top]/c[top][0])**4; normalize
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for wi,k in zip(w_top, top):
          k=int(k); i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n,k+1)
          if i0<i1:
             idx=np.arange(i0,i1)
             h[idx] += wi * a[k-idx]
             # plus double? factor irrelevant
       src=int(np.argmax(h)); dst=int(np.argmin(h))
       if src==dst or a[src]<=1e-14: break? perturb
       improved=False
       for frac in (0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1):
          amt=a[src]*frac
          trial=a.copy(); trial[src]-=amt; trial[dst]+=amt; trial=np.maximum(trial,0); trial=norm_sum1(trial)
          M2=lin_conv_max(trial)
          if M2 < M - 1e-15:
             a=trial; improved=True; break
       if improved: stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 20 ==0:
             # random micro moves
             for _ in range(5):
                i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i!=j and a[i]>1e-12:
                   frac=rng.uniform(0.001,0.01); amt=a[i]*frac; trial=...
                   M2=...
                   if M2<M: accept
          if stall>500: break
       it++
    return best
```
This may be slow due many FFTs but final small time. Use top 10.

Now, coordinate polish: It can be useful for final. Let's implement `coordinate_polish` with target not needed. Use top 15 and pair moves. Need avoid O(n) loop for each k too slow. We can compute h via weights top 15 using loop over k O(15n) Python indexing, okay. Then pair move. For each move, evaluate FFT. Fractions list maybe 5. If no improve 200 stalls, break.

```
def coordinate_polish(a0,time_limit,verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); best=a; bestM=lin_conv_max(a); t0; rng; stall=0; no_improve=0
    while time...:
      c=lin_conv(a); M=c.max(); if M<bestM...
      top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:15]
      w=(c[top]/c[top][0])**4; w/=w.sum()
      h=np.zeros(n)
      for wi,k in zip(w,top):
         k=int(k); i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n,k+1)
         if i0<i1:
            # h[i] += wi * 2*a[k-i]
            idx=np.arange(i0,i1)
            h[idx] += wi * a[k-idx]
      src=int(np.argmax(h)); dst=int(np.argmin(h))
      if src==dst:
         # random dest
         dst=int(rng.integers(n))
      improved=False
      if a[src] > 1e-14:
         for frac in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05):
            amt=a[src]*frac
            trial=a.copy(); trial[src]-=amt; trial[dst]+=amt
            trial=np.maximum(trial,0); trial=norm_sum1(trial)
            M2=lin_conv_max(trial)
            if M2 < M - 1e-15:
                a=trial; improved=True; break
      if improved: stall=0; no_improve=0
      else:
         stall+=1; no_improve+=1
         if no_improve > 30:
             # random moves
             for _ in range(3):
                i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i!=j and a[i]>1e-12:
                   frac=float(rng.uniform(0.001,0.01)); amt=a[i]*frac; trial...
                   M2=...
                   if M2<M: accept; no_improve=0; break
         if stall > 100: break
    return best
```
The h should include all top lags, but pair move source/dest based on one move. Good.

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2 for a>=0.

The optimizer combines several complementary ideas:
  - FFT-based exact objective evaluation.
  - Newton-style alternating projection between a and a clipped/box-projected
    target convolution, using frequency-domain square-root updates aligned
    with the current iterate.
  - Mirror-descent on a soft/max weighted convolution gradient, with target
    thresholding and restarts.
  - A corrected Frank-Wolfe/LP step that minimizes the linearized quadratic
    objective max_k (2*(y*g)_k - c_k) over the simplex, with active-set
    management and line search.
  - Local coordinate mass-transfer polishing.
  - Interpolation to longer lengths when beneficial.

The returned list is the best sequence found before the time limit expires.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_MODULE_SOLVER = None


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def safe_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return a


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = safe_arr(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or a.size == 0:
        n = max(1, len(a))
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return a / s


def lin_conv(a):
    a = safe_arr(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.asarray(np.real_if_close(c, tol=1000), dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def lin_conv_max(a):
    c = lin_conv(a)
    return float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0


def local_score(a):
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    if len(a) == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(len(a)) * lin_conv_max(a)


def official_score(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if a.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = safe_arr(f)
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    if old_n == 0 or new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(new_n)), dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / new_n)
    fn = f / s
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, fn)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-15:
        out = np.full(new_n, 1.0 / new_n)
    else:
        out /= ss
    return out


def project_box_sum(c, m):
    """Project c>=0,sum=1 onto {p>=0,sum=1, max(p)<=m} in an L2-ish way."""
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or c.size == 0:
        return np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = c / s
    m = float(m)
    if m <= 0.0:
        return c
    if c.max() <= m * (1.0 + 1e-12):
        return c
    if m * float(c.size) <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        # Infeasible target; return the flattest possible vector.
        return np.full_like(c, 1.0 / c.size)

    lo = float(c.min()) - m - max(1.0, float(c.max()))
    hi = float(c.max()) + max(1.0, float(c.max()))
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(c - mid, 0.0, m)
        sp = float(p.sum())
        if sp > 1.0:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    p = np.clip(c - lo, 0.0, m)
    sp = float(p.sum())
    if sp <= 1e-15:
        p = np.full_like(c, 1.0 / c.size)
    else:
        p = p / sp

    p = np.minimum(p, m)
    sp = float(p.sum())
    if sp <= 1e-15:
        p = np.full_like(c, 1.0 / c.size)
    else:
        p = p / sp
    return p


def newton_improve(a0, time_limit, target_score, verbose=True):
    """
    Alternate:
      1. c = a*a
      2. clip/project c toward a feasible target max=m
      3. take a damped Newton square-root step in Fourier domain
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best = a.copy()
    bestM = lin_conv_max(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(101)
    frac = 2.0e-4
    stall = 0
    eps_base = 1e-10
    rho_values = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.07, 0.03)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        c = lin_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-15:
            bestM = M
            best = a.copy()

        target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        if M <= target_M * (1.0 + 1e-12):
            # Once the requested target is met, try to squeeze a little more.
            target_M = max(0.0, M - 1.0e-5 * M)

        m_target = min(M * (1.0 - frac), target_M)
        if m_target <= 0.0 or m_target >= M:
            m_target = M * max(1.0 - frac, 0.9999)

        c_target = project_box_sum(c, m_target)
        C = np.fft.fft(c_target, N)
        A = np.fft.fft(a, N)

        # Damped Newton square-root steps, aligned with current phase.
        for _ in range(2):
            absA = np.abs(A)
            maxA = max(1.0, float(absA.max()) if absA.size else 1.0)
            eps = eps_base * maxA
            small = absA < eps

            if np.any(small):
                phase = np.ones(N, dtype=np.complex128)
                nz = absA > 0.0
                phase[nz] = A[nz] / absA[nz]
                A_safe = A.copy()
                A_safe[small] = eps * phase[small]
            else:
                A_safe = A

            A = 0.5 * (A + C / A_safe)
            # Damp slightly to avoid phase jumps.
            # (Would be equivalent to doing half-Newton again if needed.)
            A[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j

        b_full = np.fft.ifft(A).real
        a_cand = b_full[:n]
        a_cand = np.maximum(a_cand, 0.0)
        ss = float(a_cand.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            a_cand = a.copy()
        else:
            a_cand = a_cand / ss

        accepted = False
        curM = M
        for rho in rho_values:
            trial = (1.0 - rho) * a + rho * a_cand
            trial = norm_sum1(trial)
            M2 = lin_conv_max(trial)
            if M2 < curM - 1e-15:
                a = trial
                curM = M2
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if curM < bestM - 1e-15:
                bestM = curM
                best = a.copy()
                if curM > target_M:
                    frac = max(frac * 0.85, 5.0e-6)
                else:
                    frac = max(frac * 0.6, 2.0e-6)
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall < 6:
                frac = min(frac * 1.8, 8.0e-3)
            else:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n) * a
                a = norm_sum1(a + noise)
                frac = min(frac * 1.8, 8.0e-3)
                stall = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it == 1 or it % 50 == 0):
            print("    Newton iter=%d M=%.12e frac=%.3e" % (it, M, frac))

    return best


def weighted_gradient(a, w):
    """
    h[i] = sum_j a[j] w[i+j], for i=0..n-1.
    This is the (unnormalized) gradient contribution from weights w on lags.
    """
    a = safe_arr(a)
    w = safe_arr(w)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    N = next_pow2(3 * n)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    W = np.fft.fft(w, N)
    h = np.fft.ifft(np.conj(A) * W).real[:n]
    return h


def mirror_improve(a0, time_limit, target_score, q_start=1.5, q_end=80.0,
                   step0=0.18, verbose=True):
    """
    Mirror descent on log(a) using soft-thresholded peak weights.
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)

    best = a.copy()
    bestM = lin_conv_max(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(202)
    eta = max(1e-4, float(step0))
    q = float(q_start)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    step_factors = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.07)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        expa = np.exp(loga - loga.max())
        sa = float(expa.sum())
        if sa <= 1e-300:
            a = norm_sum1(best)
            loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
            continue
        a = expa / sa

        c = lin_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-15:
            bestM = M
            best = a.copy()

        target_base = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        if M <= target_base * (1.0 + 1e-12):
            # Try to keep improving below the requested target.
            target_M = max(0.0, M - 1.0e-5 * M)
        else:
            target_M = target_base

        floor = target_M if target_M < M else M * (1.0 - 5.0e-3)
        floor = max(0.0, min(floor, M * (1.0 - 1.0e-8)))

        w = np.maximum(c - floor, 0.0)
        w = w ** q
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w = (c / max(M, 1e-300)) ** q
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 1e-300:
                w = np.zeros_like(c)
                top = int(np.argmax(c))
                w[top] = 1.0
                ws = 1.0
        w = w / ws

        h = weighted_gradient(a, w)
        g = h - float(np.mean(h))
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
        if scale <= 1e-15:
            break
        g = g / scale

        accepted = False
        best_trial = a
        best_trial_M = M
        for sf in step_factors:
            new_loga = loga - eta * sf * g
            new_loga = np.clip(new_loga, -50.0, 50.0)
            na = np.exp(new_loga - new_loga.max())
            sn = float(na.sum())
            if sn <= 1e-300:
                continue
            na = na / sn
            M2 = lin_conv_max(na)
            if M2 < M - 1e-15:
                loga = new_loga
                a = na
                best_trial = na
                best_trial_M = M2
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if best_trial_M < bestM - 1e-15:
                bestM = best_trial_M
                best = best_trial.copy()
            eta = min(eta * 1.04, 3.0)
            q = max(float(q_start), q * 0.92)
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            eta = max(eta * 0.80, 1e-5)
            q = min(q * 1.15, float(q_end))
            if stall > 120:
                base = best if bestM <= M else a
                base = norm_sum1(base)
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.015, base.size) * base
                a = norm_sum1(base + noise)
                loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
                loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
                eta = max(1e-4, float(step0))
                q = float(q_start)
                stall = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it == 1 or it % 100 == 0):
            print("    Mirror iter=%d M=%.12e q=%.2f eta=%.4f" % (it, M, q, eta))

    return best


def cross_conv(a, b):
    a = safe_arr(a)
    b = safe_arr(b)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:size]
    return np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)


def get_solver():
    global _MODULE_SOLVER
    if _MODULE_SOLVER is not None:
        return _MODULE_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None

    candidates = []
    for name in ["CLARABEL", "PDLP", "ECOS", "SCS"]:
        sol = getattr(cp, name, None)
        if sol is not None:
            candidates.append(sol)

    for sol in candidates:
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(8, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.6])
            prob.solve(solver=sol, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _MODULE_SOLVER = sol
                print("  LP solver: " + str(sol))
                return sol
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        x = cp.Variable(8, nonneg=True)
        prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.6])
        prob.solve(verbose=False)
        if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
            print("  LP solver: default")
            return None  # We require a named solver for repeated use.
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None


def shifted_lp_fw(a0, time_limit, solver, K=220, maxK=520, verbose=True):
    """
    Frank-Wolfe-like LP step with the correct linearized objective:
        min_g max_k [2*(y*g)_k - (y*y)_k]
    s.t. g>=0, sum(g)=1.
    """
    if solver is None:
        return norm_sum1(a0)

    y = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = y.size
    if n < 30:
        return y

    best = y.copy()
    bestM = lin_conv_max(y)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    score = None
    active_set = None
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    K_cur = min(max(50, int(K)), maxK, max(60, n // 6))
    maxK_eff = min(maxK, max(120, n // 4))

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        c = lin_conv(y)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-14:
            bestM = M
            best = y.copy()

        target_K = min(maxK_eff, K_cur)

        if active_set is None:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:target_K]
            active_set = set(int(k) for k in idx)
            score = c.copy()
        else:
            # Decay old scores; keep important lags.
            score = 0.85 * score + 0.15 * c
            cand = set()
            idx1 = np.argsort(score)[::-1][:2 * target_K]
            for k in idx1:
                cand.add(int(k))
            idx2 = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:target_K]
            for k in idx2:
                cand.add(int(k))

            if len(cand) > target_K:
                arr = np.array(sorted(cand), dtype=np.int64)
                vals = score[arr]
                order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:target_K]
                active_set = set(int(arr[i]) for i in order)
            else:
                active_set = cand

        active_list = sorted(active_set)
        if not active_list:
            break
        active_list = [int(k) for k in active_list if 0 <= k < len(c)]
        if not active_list:
            break

        A = np.zeros((len(active_list), n), dtype=np.float64)
        c_act = np.array([c[k] for k in active_list], dtype=np.float64)

        for jj, k in enumerate(active_list):
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n, k + 1)
            if i0 < i1:
                start = k - i0
                stop = k - i1
                A[jj, i0:i1] = 2.0 * y[start:stop:-1]

        g = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
        t = cp.Variable()
        constraints = [
            cp.sum(g) == 1,
            A @ g - t <= c_act,
            t >= -M
        ]
        prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), constraints)

        try:
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        except Exception:
            prob = None

        ok = False
        if prob is not None and prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
            gv = g.value
            if gv is not None:
                try:
                    gval = np.asarray(gv, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
                    gval = np.maximum(gval, 0.0)
                    sg = float(gval.sum())
                    if sg > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(sg):
                        gval /= sg
                        ok = True
                except Exception:
                    gval = None

        if not ok:
            # Shrink active set and try again.
            K_cur = max(80, int(K_cur * 0.6))
            active_set = None
            continue

        conv_yg = cross_conv(y, gval)
        pred = 2.0 * conv_yg - c

        alphas = (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, max(0.01, 2.0 / (it + 2.0)), 0.05)
        best_trial = y
        best_trial_M = M
        for alpha in alphas:
            trial = (1.0 - alpha) * y + alpha * gval
            trial = norm_sum1(trial)
            M2 = lin_conv_max(trial)
            if M2 < best_trial_M - 1e-15:
                best_trial = trial
                best_trial_M = M2

        if best_trial_M < M - 1e-15:
            y = best_trial
            if best_trial_M < bestM - 1e-14:
                bestM = best_trial_M
                best = y.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            score = np.maximum(0.80 * score + 0.20 * c, np.maximum(pred, 0.0))
            K_cur = min(maxK_eff, K_cur + 8)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            score = np.maximum(0.75 * score + 0.25 * c, np.maximum(pred, 0.0))
            if no_improve > 35:
                K_cur = max(80, int(K_cur * 0.65))
                active_set = None
            if no_improve > 350:
                break

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it == 1 or it % 20 == 0):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            J = 2.0 * float(n) * bestM
            print("    LP-FW iter=%d t=%.1fs J=%.10f |S|=%d" % (it, elapsed, J, K_cur))

    return best


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    bestM = lin_conv_max(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(314)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        c = lin_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-15:
            bestM = M
            best = a.copy()

        top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        top = top[:min(18, len(c))]
        if top.size == 0:
            break

        ctop = c[top]
        base = ctop.max()
        if base <= 0:
            break
        w = (ctop / base) ** 4.0
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            continue
        w = w / ws

        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wi, k in zip(w, top):
            k = int(k)
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n, k + 1)
            if i0 < i1:
                idx = np.arange(i0, i1)
                h[idx] += wi * a[k - idx]

        src = int(np.argmax(h))
        dst = int(np.argmin(h))
        if src == dst:
            dst = int(rng.integers(n))

        improved = False
        if src != dst and a[src] > 1e-14:
            for frac in (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05):
                amt = a[src] * frac
                trial = a.copy()
                trial[src] -= amt
                trial[dst] += amt
                trial = norm_sum1(trial)
                M2 = lin_conv_max(trial)
                if M2 < M - 1e-15:
                    a = trial
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                accepted_rand = False
                for _ in range(5):
                    i = int(rng.integers(n))
                    j = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if i == j or a[i] <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.012))
                    amt = a[i] * frac
                    trial = a.copy()
                    trial[i] -= amt
                    trial[j] += amt
                    trial = norm_sum1(trial)
                    M2 = lin_conv_max(trial)
                    if M2 < M - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        accepted_rand = True
                        break
                if accepted_rand:
                    stall = 0
            if stall > 120:
                break

        if verbose and (stall % 200 == 0):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            J = 2.0 * float(n) * bestM
            print("    Coord t=%.1fs J=%.10f stall=%d" % (elapsed, J, stall))

    return best


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """Main entry point. Returns a list of nonnegative heights."""
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(float(budget_s) - 45.0, 1755.0)
    if time_budget <= 0:
        time_budget = 1.0

    print("Search budget: %.1f seconds" % time_budget)

    # Load parent sequence if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0 and p.sum() > 1e-10:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n0 = 6000
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
        a1 = np.exp(-0.8 * x)
        a2 = np.exp(-1.5 * x) + 0.35 * np.exp(1.5 * (x - 1.0))
        a3 = np.ones(n0)
        parent = norm_sum1(0.55 * a1 + 0.30 * a2 + 0.15 * a3)
        parent = a1 / a1.sum()  # start asymmetric; optimizers will improve.

    best_seq = norm_sum1(parent)
    best_local = local_score(best_seq)
    best_official = official_score(best_seq)
    best_official_seq = best_seq.copy()
    print("Initial: n=%d local=%.10f official=%.10f" %
          (len(best_seq), best_local, best_official))

    # Quick diverse starts.
    starts = [best_seq]
    n0 = best_seq.size
    if n0 > 200:
        starts.append(best_seq[::-1])
        kern = min(7, max(3, n0 // 1000))
        k = np.ones(kern) / float(kern)
        sm = np.convolve(best_seq, k, mode="same")
        starts.append(norm_sum1(sm))
        uni = np.full(n0, 1.0 / n0)
        starts.append(norm_sum1(0.75 * best_seq + 0.25 * uni))
        starts.append(norm_sum1(0.85 * best_seq + 0.15 * uni))
        rng0 = np.random.default_rng(7)
        noise = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.04 * rng0.standard_normal(n0), 0.05)
        starts.append(norm_sum1(noise * best_seq))

    for s in starts:
        s = norm_sum1(s)
        ls = local_score(s)
        if ls < best_local - 1e-12:
            best_local = ls
            best_seq = s.copy()

    off0 = official_score(best_seq)
    if np.isfinite(off0) and off0 < best_official:
        best_official = off0
        best_official_seq = best_seq.copy()
    print("After starts: n=%d local=%.10f official=%.10f" %
          (len(best_seq), best_local, best_official))

    solver = get_solver()
    if solver is None:
        print("No LP solver available; skipping LP phase.")

    current_target = 1.5026
    if np.isfinite(best_official) and best_official < current_target:
        current_target = max(1.45, best_official - 0.0008)

    def refresh_target():
        nonlocal current_target
        if np.isfinite(best_official) and best_official < current_target:
            current_target = max(1.45, best_official - 0.0006)

    def update_from_candidate(cand, label):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_local, best_official, best_official_seq
        if cand is None:
            return
        cand = norm_sum1(cand)
        if cand.size == 0:
            return
        loc = local_score(cand)
        if loc < best_local - 1e-12:
            best_local = loc
            best_seq = cand.copy()
        off = official_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
            best_official = off
            best_official_seq = cand.copy()
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
            print("*** Official improved [%s]: %.10f (n=%d, local=%.10f, t=%.1f)" %
                  (label, off, len(cand), loc, elapsed))
        elif loc < best_local + 1e-4:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
            print("    [%s] local=%.10f (n=%d) t=%.1f" % (label, loc, len(cand), elapsed))

    # Phase 1: Newton clip-root
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 120:
        t1 = min(remaining * 0.22, 260.0)
        if t1 > 20:
            print("\n=== Phase 1: Newton clip-root (n=%d) ===" % len(best_seq))
            cand = newton_improve(best_seq, t1, current_target, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "newton1")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 2: Mirror descent
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 120:
        t2 = min(remaining * 0.26, 330.0)
        if t2 > 25:
            n_cur = len(best_seq)
            if n_cur <= 20000:
                print("\n=== Phase 2: Mirror descent (n=%d) ===" % n_cur)
                cand = mirror_improve(best_seq, t2, current_target,
                                      q_start=1.0, q_end=60.0, step0=0.18,
                                      verbose=True)
                update_from_candidate(cand, "mirror1")
                refresh_target()

    # Phase 3: Corrected LP-FW
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    n_cur = len(best_seq)
    if remaining > 180 and solver is not None and n_cur <= 8500:
        t3 = min(remaining * 0.28, 430.0)
        if t3 > 40:
            print("\n=== Phase 3: Shifted LP-FW (n=%d) ===" % n_cur)
            cand = shifted_lp_fw(best_seq, t3, solver, K=210, maxK=520, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "lpfw1")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 4: Newton again with lower target
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 100:
        t4 = min(remaining * 0.16, 200.0)
        if t4 > 15:
            print("\n=== Phase 4: Newton clip-root lower target (n=%d) ===" % len(best_seq))
            cand = newton_improve(best_seq, t4, current_target, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "newton2")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 5: Mirror high-q polish
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 80:
        t5 = min(remaining * 0.12, 160.0)
        if t5 > 15:
            print("\n=== Phase 5: Mirror high-q polish (n=%d) ===" % len(best_seq))
            cand = mirror_improve(best_seq, t5, current_target,
                                  q_start=8.0, q_end=120.0, step0=0.08,
                                  verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "mirror2")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 6: Longer sequences
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    n_cur = len(best_seq)
    if remaining > 180 and n_cur > 1000:
        longer_lengths = [
            int(n_cur * 1.25),
            int(n_cur * 1.55),
            int(n_cur * 1.9)
        ]
        longer_lengths = sorted(set([max(2000, ll) for ll in longer_lengths]))
        longer_lengths = [ll for ll in longer_lengths if ll > n_cur and ll <= 13000][:3]

        if longer_lengths:
            print("\n=== Phase 6: Longer sequences ===")
            for new_n in longer_lengths:
                remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
                if remaining < 120:
                    break
                a_long = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, new_n)
                loc_long = local_score(a_long)
                off_long = official_score(a_long)
                if np.isfinite(off_long) and off_long < best_official:
                    best_official = off_long
                    best_official_seq = a_long.copy()
                    best_seq = a_long.copy()
                    best_local = min(best_local, loc_long)
                    print("*** Longer n=%d official=%.10f" % (new_n, off_long))
                elif loc_long < best_local - 1e-10:
                    best_seq = a_long.copy()
                    best_local = loc_long

                # Polish longer with mirror (usually cheap enough).
                rem2 = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
                t6 = min(rem2 * 0.45, 180.0)
                if t6 > 25 and new_n <= 13000:
                    cand = mirror_improve(a_long, t6, current_target,
                                          q_start=2.0, q_end=80.0, step0=0.12,
                                          verbose=False)
                    update_from_candidate(cand, "long_mirror_n%d" % new_n)
                    refresh_target()

                rem2 = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
                t7 = min(rem2 * 0.45, 150.0)
                if t7 > 20 and new_n <= 11000:
                    cand = newton_improve(best_seq, t7, current_target, verbose=False)
                    update_from_candidate(cand, "long_newton_n%d" % new_n)
                    refresh_target()

                # If the longer one is best, continue; otherwise keep best for next.
                # best_seq already tracks local best; best_official_seq tracks official best.

    # Phase 7: Coordinate polish on current best
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 60 and len(best_seq) <= 20000:
        t8 = min(remaining * 0.35, 160.0)
        if t8 > 15:
            print("\n=== Phase 7: Coordinate polish (n=%d) ===" % len(best_seq))
            cand = coordinate_polish(best_seq, t8, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "coord1")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 8: Final squeezed Newton if time remains
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 60 and len(best_seq) <= 15000:
        t9 = min(remaining * 0.55, 220.0)
        if t9 > 20:
            # Use a still-lower target to squeeze.
            squeeze_target = current_target - 0.0012
            if not np.isfinite(squeeze_target) or squeeze_target < 1.35:
                squeeze_target = 1.40
            print("\n=== Phase 8: Final Newton squeeze (n=%d) target=%.5f ===" %
                  (len(best_seq), squeeze_target))
            cand = newton_improve(best_seq, t9, squeeze_target, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "newton_final")
            refresh_target()

    # Phase 9: Final coordinate if time remains
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 40 and len(best_seq) <= 15000:
        t10 = min(remaining * 0.70, 100.0)
        if t10 > 10:
            print("\n=== Phase 9: Final coordinate polish (n=%d) ===" % len(best_seq))
            cand = coordinate_polish(best_seq, t10, verbose=True)
            update_from_candidate(cand, "coord_final")
            refresh_target()

    # Choose final sequence: prefer official best, fall back to local best.
    if best_official_seq is None or len(best_official_seq) == 0:
        out_seq = best_seq
    else:
        out_seq = best_official_seq

    out_seq = norm_sum1(out_seq)

    # One last official check.
    final_official = official_score(out_seq)
    final_local = local_score(out_seq)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    print("\nFINAL local=%.10f official=%.10f n=%d t=%.1f" %
          (final_local, final_official, len(out_seq), elapsed))

    if not np.isfinite(final_official):
        # Extremely defensive fallback.
        n_fb = max(1000, int(len(parent)) if parent is not None else 4000)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_fb)
        out_seq = norm_sum1(np.exp(-0.7 * x) + 0.25 * x + 0.15)
        final_official = official_score(out_seq)

    return [float(x) for x in out_seq]
```
